Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a hilbert algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 139. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 608/3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 139 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the hilbert structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 608. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the hilbert structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 380. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 53 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on hilbert structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 380. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 53. QED.
